### Bug Fixes

- **`HF_API_KEY` env var not being checked** — the HF token lookup chain only checked `HF_TOKEN` → `HUGGING_FACE_HUB_TOKEN`. Users who set `HF_API_KEY` instead of `HF_TOKEN` (a common mistake) got no token detection → whoami probe failed → auto-detection didn't fire → no warning + no whitelist enforcement. Fixed: the lookup chain now checks `HF_TOKEN` → `HUGGING_FACE_HUB_TOKEN` → `HF_API_KEY` (3-way fallback) in both `config.py` (module-level constant) and `huggingface.py` (`__init__` runtime lookup).

- **Probe script pricing unit conversion bug** — the HF Router probe multiplied raw pricing values by 1e6, displaying `$0.03/1M` as `$30,000/1M`. The HF Router returns pricing in **USD per 1M tokens** (values like `0.03`, `0.42`, `3.0`), while OpenRouter returns pricing in **USD per token** (values like `0.000003`). The `*1e6` multiplication was correct for OpenRouter but wrong for HF. Fixed: HF probe now displays raw values directly.

- **HF catalog had 15 dead models** — pruned from 31 → 18 (16 live + 3 genuinely $0/token models added). Removed: all Mistral variants (entire family rotated out), all Phi variants (Phi-3.5-mini/MoE, Phi-4-mini), older Gemma 2 variants (superseded by Gemma 3), Llama 3.2 small variants (1B/3B — Llama 3.1-8B and 3.3-70B still served), Qwen legacy variants (superseded by Qwen3), CohereForAI Command R variants (org renamed to `CohereLabs/`), and zai-org/GLM-Z1-32B-0414 (replaced by newer GLM variants).

- **ZAI catalog had 6 dead models** — pruned from 17 → 13. Removed: glm-4-plus, glm-4-air, glm-4-flash, glm-4, glm-4-long, glm-4-flashx, glm-4-flashx-glm (all GLM-4 legacy variants not in live `/api/paas/v4/models`), glm-z1-air, glm-z1-flash, glm-z1-32b-0414 (Z1 reasoning variants rotated out). `glm-4.5-flash` and `glm-4.7-flash` were initially pruned but **restored** — they're accessible by model ID despite not being listed in the `/models` endpoint (the ZAI `/models` endpoint returns a subset of available models, not the full list).

- **OpenAI catalog had 2 dead models** — removed `gpt-5.6-cyber` (Daybreak model not in live API) and `gpt-rosalind-research` (restricted-access, not returned for standard API keys).

- **OpenAI whitelist had non-free models** — emptied entirely (6 → 0). Previous whitelist included `gpt-6-luna` ($0.10/$0.50 per 1M), `gpt-4o-mini` ($0.15/$0.60), `gpt-4.1-mini`, `gpt-realtime-2.1-mini`, `gpt-4o-mini-transcribe`, `gpt-transcribe`. None of these are genuinely $0/token — they all consume the $1 trial credit and $10/mo API credit at their per-token rates. VTSTech's trial credit was exhausted by `gpt-4o-mini` despite being whitelisted, proving that "low cost" is not "free". `OPENAI_FREE_ONLY=true` now rejects ALL OpenAI models with: *"OpenAI has no genuinely free ($0/token) models — every model has per-token pricing that consumes your trial credit ($1) and monthly API credit ($10). Set OPENAI_FREE_ONLY=false or use --backend hf."*

- **HF whitelist redefined as strictly $0/token** — replaced the 16-model "low-cost" whitelist with 3 genuinely $0/token models verified via live API probe: `prism-ml/Ternary-Bonsai-27B-gguf` ($0/$0 via Together), `prism-ml/Ternary-Bonsai-27B-AWQ-4bit` ($0/$0 via Together), `inclusionAI/Ling-3.0-flash-Fin` ($0/$0 via Novita). These are the only models on the HF Router with `pricing: {input: 0, output: 0}` on at least one partner provider. The `is_free` flag is `false` for all 336 combos on HF (the flag is conservative), but the pricing data is the ground truth. `HF_FREE_FALLBACK_MODEL` default updated from the dead `Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-1M` → `prism-ml/Ternary-Bonsai-27B-gguf` ($0/token).

- **`generate_completions_stream()` bypassed `OPENAI_FREE_ONLY` upfront check** — same lesson learned on HF (R07.02 polish): the agentic loop calls `generate_completions_stream()` directly for cloud backends (`is_cloud=True`), NOT `generate_stream()`. The initial OpenAI scaffold only overrode `generate_stream()`. Added the `generate_completions_stream()` override so the whitelist check fires in the streaming path the agentic loop actually uses.

## [R07.02] - 2026-09-26 12:09 AM

First-party **Hugging Face Inference Router** backend — the third cloud-provider plugin (after ZAI and OpenRouter) and the first to ship with a dedicated API Technical Reference written *before* the implementation, as the blueprint. The HF Router at `https://router.huggingface.co/v1` proxies 100+ open-weight models (Llama, Qwen, DeepSeek, Mistral, Gemma, GLM, Phi, Command-R, gpt-oss) served by ~18 partner providers (Together, Groq, Novita, DeepInfra, Fireworks, Cerebras, Replicate, Fal AI, Featherless, Baseten, Cohere, Nscale, OVHcloud, Public AI, Scaleway, WaveSpeedAI, Z.ai, HF Inference) through a single OpenAI-compatible `/chat/completions` endpoint. The HF Router differs from OpenRouter in one important way for users: it doesn't use OpenRouter's `:free` model-id suffix convention — free-tier status is determined by account credit + provider routing, so the AgentKthx `HF_FREE_ONLY` env var enforces a curated **`HF_FREE_MODEL_WHITELIST`** (31 open-weight models verified to have free-tier access via partner providers) and auto-appends the `:cheapest` routing suffix. HTTP 402 (free-tier credit exhausted) is a hard failure in strict mode (no retry — retrying burns router quota) and triggers `HF_FREE_FALLBACK_MODEL` swap in permissive mode, mirroring the ZAI plugin's 429 insufficient-balance fallback pattern at `zai.py:706-714`. The scaffold was built end-to-end against the blueprint in `docs/HUGGINGFACE_API_TECHNICAL_REFERENCE.md` (committed in R07.01); the parallel `docs/OPENAI_API_TECHNICAL_REFERENCE.md` (also R07.01) will serve the same role for the planned R07.0x OpenAI plugin. Initial scaffold shipped with suite 984 → 1024 passed. The R07.02 polish (see "### Polish — Live API Integration" below) added a further +32 tests → **1056 passed, 9 skipped, 0 failures** (+72 total new tests across the release: 71 in `tests/test_huggingface_backend.py`, 1 in `tests/test_is_cloud_attribute.py`).

### Features

- **New `huggingface` backend plugin (1685 lines)** — `agentkthx/plugins/huggingface/{__init__.py, plugin.json, huggingface.py}` implements `HuggingFaceBackend(OpenAICompatibleBackend)` with the full feature set from the API Technical Reference: HF_TOKEN Bearer auth (with `HUGGING_FACE_HUB_TOKEN` fallback for users still on the older `huggingface_hub` SDK convention); provider routing via `:fastest`/`:cheapest`/`:preferred`/`:<provider-name>` suffix on the model id (controlled by the new `HF_PROVIDER_POLICY` env var); `HF_FREE_ONLY` strict whitelist enforcement that rejects non-whitelisted models BEFORE any HTTP request is made (verified by the `test_free_only_true_rejects_non_whitelisted_model` test — 0 calls to `_make_api_request`); `HF_FREE_FALLBACK_MODEL` swap on HTTP 402 (mirrors ZAI's credit-exhaustion fallback); 429 retry loop with `Retry-After` header honor + exponential back-off schedule (5s → 10s → 20s → 40s → 80s → 90s cap with ±20% jitter, 6 retries default — mirrors OpenRouter R06.54); ReAct fallback when a partner provider rejects the `tools` field (HF-specific error patterns added: `tool use is not supported`, `tool_calls not supported on this model`, plus the TGI/llama-server form `unsupported param: tools`); reasoning-content capture for thinking-capable HF models (Qwen3-Thinking family, DeepSeek-R1, openai/gpt-oss-20b-reasoning) via the `reasoning` field on the response message (mirrors OpenRouter's R06.53 streaming capture); context-length 400 recovery via the shared `OpenAICompatibleBackend._handle_context_length_400` helper (ARCH-03 R06.57 — HF Router's error format matches the base-class default regex patterns, so no override is needed); JEV dispatch via `_jev_call_completions()` routing through `self.generate()` so auth + 429 retry + `HF_FREE_ONLY` are preserved; both canonical (`huggingface`) and alias (`hf`) `--backend` values work end-to-end (verified by `agentkthx models --backend hf` returning 154 models from the live router API). The 1-hour model-list cache (mirrors OpenRouterBackend) prefers live `/v1/models` data for `context_length` and per-provider `max_completion_tokens`, falling back to a 31-model static catalog `HF_MODELS` when the API is unreachable.

### Architecture

- **`BackendType` enum: add `HUGGINGFACE = "huggingface"`** (`core/types.py`) — the value used by `HuggingFaceBackend.backend_type`. Pattern matches the existing ZAI / OPENROUTER / GEMINI entries; no other site in the codebase needed updating (R06.57 MAINT-05 already replaced the 8 hardcoded backend allowlists in `cli.py` with `getattr(backend, 'is_cloud', False)` checks, so a 4th cloud backend now picks up streaming-by-default, catalog-based defaults, cloud column layout in `cmd_models`, and `OPENAI` api_mode default with zero edits to `cli/`).
- **`config.py`: 7 new HF env-var constants + `DEFAULT_MODEL` branch** — `HF_BASE_URL` (default `https://router.huggingface.co/v1`), `HF_BASE_URL_LEGACY` (default `https://api-inference.huggingface.co` — documented in the API Technical Reference as the legacy Serverless TGI surface but NOT used by the v0.1 backend; kept for future fallback), `HF_TOKEN` (with `HUGGING_FACE_HUB_TOKEN` fallback to mirror Google's `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` precedence), `HF_DEFAULT_MODEL` (default `openai/gpt-oss-120b`), `HF_FREE_ONLY` (parsed as bool from `("1", "true", "yes")` — same pattern as `ZAI_FREE_ONLY` and `OPENROUTER_FREE_ONLY`), `HF_FREE_FALLBACK_MODEL` (default `Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-1M` — broad partner support + low token cost), `HF_PROVIDER_POLICY` (default `""` — one of `""` / `"fastest"` / `"cheapest"` / `"preferred"` / a partner name like `"groq"`). The `DEFAULT_MODEL` branch accepts both `huggingface` and `hf` so `AGENTKTHX_BACKEND=hf` works without surprise.

### Bug Fixes

- **`get_backend_choices()` silently dropped plugin-registered aliases** (`agentkthx/backends/__init__.py`) — the function called `pm.list_backend_names()` which only returns canonical names registered via `register_backend()`. Aliases declared in a plugin's `plugin.json` `cli_flags."--backend"` array (e.g. the huggingface plugin declares `["huggingface", "hf"]`) were silently dropped from the CLI's argparse choices — `agentkthx models --backend hf` failed with `invalid choice: 'hf'`. Now delegates to `pm.get_backend_choices()` which reads the `cli_flags` field directly, so all declared choices surface in the argparse choices list. The fix benefits any future plugin that wants to register an alias (previously required editing `backends/__init__.py` to add the alias to the native list — the wrong layer for a plugin-registered name).
- **`find_plugin_for_backend()` chicken-and-egg on aliases** (`agentkthx/plugins/_loader.py`) — the reverse-mapping (backend name → plugin name) only checked `manifest.provides.backends` (the canonical name). When `_ensure_plugin("hf")` was called for the alias, it failed to find a matching plugin and fell back to `plugin_name = "hf"` (which doesn't exist as a plugin directory), raising `ValueError: Cannot load backend 'hf'. Plugin 'hf' not found`. The alias map (`_backend_aliases`) is populated only AFTER `register()` runs, but `register()` runs only AFTER `_ensure_plugin()` resolves the name — classic chicken-and-egg. Now also checks `manifest.provides.cli_flags."--backend"` so aliases declared in the manifest resolve to the right plugin BEFORE the plugin's `register()` has had a chance to call `register_backend(alias_of=...)`.
- **Agentic-loop streaming path bypassed `HF_FREE_ONLY` upfront check** (`agentkthx/plugins/huggingface/huggingface.py`) — the agentic loop calls `generate_completions_stream()` directly for cloud backends (`is_cloud=True`), NOT `generate_stream()`. The original scaffold only overrode `generate_stream()` (which checks `HF_FREE_ONLY` upfront and raises `RuntimeError` for non-whitelisted models). The inherited base-class `generate_completions_stream()` skipped the whitelist check and made HTTP requests for paid models — defeating the purpose of `HF_FREE_ONLY` (which is to prevent accidental paid API calls). Caught via live CLI smoke test (`HF_FREE_ONLY=true agentkthx run --backend hf --model anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet` reached the API layer and got a 401 instead of being rejected upfront). Mirrors the ZAI backend's pattern (zai.py:756-785 overrides BOTH `generate_stream()` AND `generate_completions_stream()`). Added the missing override; verified via CLI smoke test (paid model rejected in 0ms with 0 HTTP requests made).

### Tests

- **39 new tests in `tests/test_huggingface_backend.py`** (697 lines, 8 test classes) — covers (1) `TestWhitelistAndCatalog`: `HF_FREE_MODEL_WHITELIST` is non-empty, `HF_MODELS` catalog covers every whitelisted model (no orphans), the headline open-weight families are present; (2) `TestIsFreeModel`: 7 cases covering base id, all suffix forms (`:fastest`/`:cheapest`/`:<provider>`), paid models with/without suffix, unknown models; (3) `TestHasProviderSuffix`: 4 cases on the `:` detector; (4) `TestApplyProviderPolicy`: 3 cases — env-policy application, explicit-suffix precedence, provider-name preservation; (5) `TestBackendHooks`: `_get_chat_completions_url()` returns the HF Router path, `_get_auth_headers()` includes Bearer + Content-Type but NOT the OpenRouter-specific `HTTP-Referer`/`X-Title` (HF has no leaderboard system), `_iter_sse_lines` is implemented; (6) `TestIsCloudAndBackendType`: `is_cloud` inherits `True` (R06.57 MAINT-05 allows the new cloud backend without `cli/` edits), `backend_type` is `BackendType.HUGGINGFACE`; (7) `TestIsToolsNotSupportedError`: parity with all OpenRouter-shared patterns, plus the HF-specific additions (`tool use is not supported`, `tool_calls not supported on this model`, `unsupported param: tools`); specificity check that unrelated errors (Internal server error, Rate limit, Invalid model, context-length 400) do NOT trigger the ReAct fallback; (8) `TestGenerateFlow`: successful tool-calling generate parses content + tool_calls + usage + finish_reason; ReAct fallback retries without `tools` when the partner rejects them; empty response raises `RuntimeError` so the chat loop surfaces a meaningful error; (9) `TestFreeOnlyEnforcement`: `HF_FREE_ONLY=false` allows paid models to reach the HTTP layer; `HF_FREE_ONLY=true` rejects non-whitelisted models with 0 HTTP requests (verified by an `AssertionError`-raising mock for `_make_api_request`); `HF_FREE_ONLY=true` allows whitelisted models and auto-appends the `:cheapest` suffix; (10) `TestCreditExhaustionFallback`: `HF_FREE_ONLY=true` 402 surfaces as a clear actionable error (no retry); (11) `TestPluginManifest`: v0.2 schema compliance, `provides.backends` maps to the right class, `cli_flags."--backend"` includes both `huggingface` and `hf`, `config.defaults` match the env-var defaults in `config.py`; (12) `TestPluginDiscovery`: integration with `PluginManager` — discovery, load, `get_backend_choices()` includes both names, `get_backend_class()` returns `HuggingFaceBackend`, unload works cleanly.
- **`tests/test_is_cloud_attribute.py`: 1 new regression test** — `test_huggingface_inherits_true` documents that the R06.57 MAINT-05 architectural choice (default `is_cloud=True` on `OpenAICompatibleBackend` subclasses) extends cleanly to a 4th cloud backend. The class now imports `HuggingFaceBackend` alongside the other 3 cloud backends (`ZaiBackend`, `OpenRouterBackend`, `GeminiBackend`) and asserts the attribute resolves to `True` with no override. This test would have failed if the v0.1 scaffold had inherited from `BaseBackend` directly (the safe-fallback path) instead of `OpenAICompatibleBackend` — keeping future contributors honest about the architecture.

### Documentation

- **The HF plugin implementation followed `docs/HUGGINGFACE_API_TECHNICAL_REFERENCE.md` (committed in R07.01) as the spec** — the reference's "Implementation Notes for AgentKthx" section (method-by-method table for `HuggingFaceBackend`, including `__init__`, `list_models`, `is_running`, `generate`, `generate_stream`, `_make_api_request`, `_stream_request`, `_build_openai_body`, `_parse_openai_response`, `_is_tools_not_supported_error`, `_is_insufficient_quota`, `_enforce_free_only`, `_detect_key_type`, `test_tool_support`, `_jev_call_completions`) was the implementation contract; the proposed `plugin.json` was copied verbatim. The 14-section reference (Authentication & Endpoint Details, Request/Response Structure, Sampling Parameters, Model Catalog & Discovery, Function Calling, Streaming, Provider Routing Preferences, Error Codes & Recovery, Rate Limiting & Concurrency, Free Tier Behavior, Multimodal Content Handling, Implementation Notes, Proposed `plugin.json`, Troubleshooting Matrix, References) covers everything the scaffold needed — and the few gaps surfaced during implementation (the agentic-loop `generate_completions_stream()` bypass, the `find_plugin_for_backend` chicken-and-egg, the `get_backend_choices` alias drop) are now documented in this changelog as bug fixes for future contributors.
- **`docs/OPENAI_API_TECHNICAL_REFERENCE.md` (committed in R07.01) awaits the planned R07.0x OpenAI plugin** — same blueprint pattern. 15 sections covering both Chat Completions (`/v1/chat/completions`) and Responses API (`/v1/responses`), Sept 2026 model lineup (GPT-6 astra/sol/luna, GPT-5.6 Daybreak sol/cyber, gpt-realtime-2.1, gpt-image-2/2.5, gpt-transcribe variants, gpt-5.3-codex, chat-latest, gpt-rosalind-research), `OPENAI_FREE_MODEL_WHITELIST` (6 very-low-cost models), service-tier matrix (`auto`/`default`/`flex`/`scale`/`priority`/`fast`), `reasoning_effort` enum (`none`→`max`), `reasoning.mode` (`standard`/`pro`), proposed `plugin.json` with 8 env vars including `OPENAI_ORGANIZATION_ID`, `OPENAI_PROJECT_ID`, `OPENAI_SERVICE_TIER`, `OPENAI_REASONING_EFFORT`.

### Polish — Live API Integration

After the initial scaffold shipped and VTSTech ran the first live `agentkthx chat --backend hf --model openai/gpt-oss-20b --stream --think` session successfully (banner: `R07.02-8a62f7a`, 154-model cache populated, reasoning content captured via the new `reasoning` field, footer token-tracking live `↑20 ↓8`), a direct probe of `GET https://router.huggingface.co/v1/models` revealed that the live API response shape is **much richer** than the technical reference documented. The initial `_parse_hf_model()` looked for flat model-level `pricing.prompt` / `pricing.completion` fields that don't exist on the live API — the real shape nests pricing under a per-provider `providers[]` array with `{input, output}` keys, plus a per-provider `is_free: bool` flag (the gold we wanted but didn't know existed until the live probe). As of 2026-09-26: `is_free` is `false` for all 336 combos even with auth — HF's free-tier model is purely credit-based ($0.10/mo credit at partner provider rates, matches the May 2026 klymentiev report). The polish captures the richer shape anyway, so when HF flips any combo `is_free=true` tomorrow (sponsored/promo window), AgentKthx auto-picks it up with zero code change. VTSTech's live `whoami-v2` response confirmed the token is valid (`name: VTSTech`, `email: veritas@vts-tech.org`, `auth.accessToken.role: read`, `canPay: false` → free tier, `billingMode: prepaid`, `periodEnd: 1790812800` → Sept 30 2026, `isPro: false`) — exactly the user profile that benefits from `HF_FREE_ONLY` enforcement. The polish adds a per-user free-tier probe so the same protection auto-enables even when the user did NOT set the env var explicitly.

- **`_parse_hf_model()` rewritten to capture the live per-provider shape** (`agentkthx/plugins/huggingface/huggingface.py`) — the parser now extracts and aggregates the per-provider fields documented in the live `/v1/models` response: `cheapest_input_per_1m` and `cheapest_output_per_1m` (USD per 1M tokens, MIN across providers — for surfacing "via $X/1M" in the model listing later, Change #4 deferred), `any_free_provider` (OR of `is_free` across providers), `any_supports_tools` / `all_support_tools` (tool-support signals per provider), `any_supports_structured_output`, `owned_by` (the model's authoring org), `provider_count`. Top-level `context_length` and `max_completion_tokens` are aggregated as the MAX across providers (best-case budget — the actual budget per request depends on which provider the router picks under `:fastest` routing, which is non-deterministic). The full raw `providers[]` array is preserved on the parsed model under the `providers` shortcut key (for `_is_free_model_live()` consultation) and `model_data` (full original response for forward-compat — any future field the API adds is available without code change). Verified live: `Qwen/Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507` now shows `256K` context in `agentkthx models --backend hf` (the per-provider max from the live API) vs the previous `32K` from the static catalog — the live API is now correctly consulted.

- **`_is_free_model_live()` instance method — live `is_free` auto-detection** (`agentkthx/plugins/huggingface/huggingface.py`) — supplements (does NOT replace) the static `HF_FREE_MODEL_WHITELIST` with live API `is_free` flag consultation. Returns True if EITHER (a) the model is in the static whitelist (fast path, no instance state needed — the documented ground truth when `/v1/models` is unreachable), OR (b) the live API cache has any provider on this model flagged `is_free=true`. As of 2026-09-26: `is_free` is `false` for all 336 combos even with auth — but HF can flip this true at any time for sponsored/promo windows, and this method auto-picks that up with zero code change. Strips the provider suffix (`:fastest` / `:cheapest` / `:preferred` / `:<provider-name>`) before lookup — the suffix is a routing hint, not part of the model identity. Falls back gracefully if cache is empty (returns False, falls back to static-whitelist-only consultation). The 9 call sites (`generate`, `generate_stream`, `generate_completions_stream`, `_make_api_request` 402 path × 2, `_iter_sse_lines` 402 path × 2, `list_models` filter) all updated to use the new live method.

- **`_probe_whoami()` + `_resolve_free_only_mode()` — token validation + free-tier auto-detection** (`agentkthx/plugins/huggingface/huggingface.py`) — on every `__init__`, the backend now probes `https://huggingface.co/api/whoami-v2` to (a) validate the token before the first chat call (catches typos / expired tokens early with a clear "your HF_TOKEN is invalid or expired" path instead of a confusing 401 mid-conversation), and (b) detect free-tier users via `canPay=False`. The probe is best-effort — failures (network down, 401 with bad token, 5xx) are swallowed; `_user_info` stays None and the backend still works (paid inference will surface its own 401 at request time). Token-role check is honest: a `read`-role fine-grained token (the default) is enough for `whoami-v2`; higher-role endpoints (`/api/inference-providers`, `/api/billing/usage`) require `write` or `admin` role and return 401 for `read`-role tokens — we don't need them anyway. `_resolve_free_only_mode()` resolves the effective `HF_FREE_ONLY` behavior with explicit-env-var > whoami-auto-detect > module-constant fallback: explicit `HF_FREE_ONLY=true` → strict (no auto-detect, respects explicit opt-in); explicit `HF_FREE_ONLY=false` → permissive (opt-out — skips whoami entirely); unset → auto-detect: `canPay=False` → strict + one-time stderr warning ("Detected free-tier account, $0.10/mo credit at partner provider rates. Auto-enabling HF_FREE_ONLY whitelist. Set HF_FREE_ONLY=false to override."), `canPay=True` → permissive; whoami unreachable → fall back to module-level `HF_FREE_ONLY` constant. The env-var check reads `os.environ` directly (not the parsed bool constant) so we can distinguish "user set the var" from "default false" — critical for the auto-detect path. VTSTech's account (`canPay=false`) gets auto-enabled whitelist protection + the warning even without setting the env var explicitly; if he later adds a billing card, the auto-detection flips to permissive with no code change.

- **`_apply_provider_policy_live(backend, model_id)` helper + `force_cheapest` parameter** (`agentkthx/plugins/huggingface/huggingface.py`) — the module-level `_apply_provider_policy(model_id, force_cheapest=False)` now takes an optional `force_cheapest` parameter (replaces the old module-level `HF_FREE_ONLY` check). The new `_apply_provider_policy_live(backend, model_id)` instance helper passes `force_cheapest=backend._free_only_effective` so the auto-detected free-tier mode is honored everywhere — `:cheapest` is auto-appended to model ids even when the user did NOT set `HF_FREE_ONLY=true` explicitly. The 9 instance call sites (`generate`, `generate_stream`, `generate_completions_stream`, `_make_api_request` 402 path × 2, `_iter_sse_lines` 402 path × 2) all updated to use the live helper. Backward-compat preserved: the old `_apply_provider_policy(model_id)` form still works (defaults `force_cheapest=False`, falls back to the module-level `HF_FREE_ONLY` constant — existing tests untouched).

- **Once-per-process warning flag** (`agentkthx/plugins/huggingface/huggingface.py`) — the free-tier stderr warning is now process-scoped via a class-level `_free_tier_warning_emitted: bool = False` flag. The CLI may instantiate `HuggingFaceBackend` twice in one command (once for `_probe_backend` feature discovery before argparse, once for the actual `cmd_models` / `cmd_chat` invocation), and the warning was firing twice — confusing the user about whether something was wrong. The flag is checked before the print, set to True on first emission, so subsequent instances in the same process skip the print but **still enforce the whitelist** (return True). Class-level (not instance-level) so all instances share the same flag. Caught during live CLI testing of the auto-detection (`unset HF_FREE_ONLY && agentkthx models --backend hf` showed the warning twice).

- **`_probe_whoami()` early-return resets `_user_info` to None** (`agentkthx/plugins/huggingface/huggingface.py`) — when `api_key` is empty, the method bailed out early via `return` but didn't reset `self._user_info = None`, leaving stale state from a previous (failed) probe. Surfaced by `test_probe_skips_when_no_api_key` — the test set `_user_info = "stale-value"` then called `_probe_whoami()` with empty api_key and expected `_user_info` to be None, but it stayed as "stale-value". Fixed: the early-return path now explicitly sets `self._user_info = None` before returning, so `_resolve_free_only_mode()` falls back cleanly to the module constant.

### Tests (Polish — Live API Integration)

- **+32 new tests across 5 new test classes** (`tests/test_huggingface_backend.py`) — (1) `TestParseHfModelShape` (8 tests): per-provider list preserved under the `providers` shortcut key, `context_length` aggregated as MAX across providers, `cheapest_input_per_1m` / `cheapest_output_per_1m` aggregated as MIN across providers, `any_free_provider` flag computed via OR across providers (with a test that flips one provider to `is_free=true` and verifies auto-detection), `any_supports_tools` / `all_support_tools` signals computed correctly, `provider_count` and `owned_by` surfaced for richer model listing, missing-`providers`-array fallback (no crash, conservative defaults), full raw `model_data` preserved for forward-compat; (2) `TestIsFreeModelLive` (7 tests): static whitelist hit returns True even without cache (fast path), whitelisted with suffix still matches (suffix stripped), non-whitelisted without cache returns False, **live `is_free=true` combo auto-detected as free** (simulates HF flipping a sponsored combo free tomorrow — the key auto-pickup test), live `is_free=false` combos return False (matches Sept 2026 state), falls back to `model_data.providers` for older cache shape, model-not-in-cache returns False; (3) `TestProbeWhoami` (4 tests): no api_key → bails early + resets `_user_info` to None (the bug-fix test), successful `whoami-v2` response populates `_user_info` with full fields (name, canPay, billingMode, periodEnd, auth.accessToken.role), network failures swallowed (no exception propagates), paid user (`canPay=true`) detected as paid → `_free_only_effective=False`; (4) `TestResolveFreeOnlyMode` (9 tests): explicit `true` overrides auto-detect, explicit `false` overrides auto-detect, unset + free tier (`canPay=false`) auto-enables with warning, unset + paid user (`canPay=true`) stays permissive, unset + whoami unreachable falls back to module constant, `yes`/`1`/`true` (case-insensitive) accepted as True, `no`/`0`/`false` accepted as False, **once-per-process warning flag** (first instance fires + sets flag, second instance skips print but still returns True), **flag-clearing re-enables warning** (clearing the flag re-fires the warning on next call — verifies the flag is the only thing suppressing it); (5) `TestApplyProviderPolicyLiveForceCheapest` (4 tests): `force_cheapest=True` appends `:cheapest`, `force_cheapest=False` no suffix when no env policy, explicit user suffix wins over `force_cheapest=True`, `_apply_provider_policy_live` passes `force_cheapest` from `_free_only_effective` (auto-detected mode is honored). Module-level `setUpModule` / `tearDownModule` mocks `_probe_whoami` to a no-op for all tests (avoids 5s network timeout on the fake token) — the mock is restored per-test in `TestProbeWhoami` via direct attribute write so the real method can be exercised with mocked `urlopen`. Suite 1024 → **1056 passed, 9 skipped, 0 failures** (+32 new tests; +72 total new tests across R07.02 including the initial scaffold).

## [R07.01] - 2026-09-25 2:41:54 PM

### CI / Infrastructure

- **Add coverage measurement (audit ARCH-02)** — `pytest-cov>=4.0` is now in the dev extras; a new `[tool.coverage]` block in `pyproject.toml` declares `source = ["agentkthx"]`, omits `agentkthx/examples/*` (demo scripts, not package code), `agentkthx/plugins/test-plugin/*` (a harness-validation stub), and the build-generated `agentkthx/_git_meta.py` (so the reported number reflects the testable surface), and configures `show_missing = true` plus `exclude_lines` for `pragma: no cover`, `raise NotImplementedError`, `if __name__ == .__main__.:`, and `if TYPE_CHECKING:`. A new `coverage` job in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs `pytest --cov=agentkthx --cov-report=term-missing --cov-report=xml:coverage.xml` once per push on Python 3.12 and uploads `coverage.xml` as a 30-day-retention build artifact via `actions/upload-artifact@v4`. The `coverage` job runs in parallel with the fast `test` matrix (no `needs:` dependency) so the existing ~2.5s PR-feedback path is preserved; the cov-instrumented run takes ~5s. Baseline recorded: **42.7% line coverage** over 13,580 measured statements (5,799 covered, 7,781 missing) across 86 files — lowest-coverage hotspots documented in `audit/audit.md` (the ARCH-02 entry) to guide TEST-01 investment. No threshold is enforced yet (deliberate — start with visibility, gate later once the baseline is stable and the omit list is trusted). The `coverage.xml` artifact is the format Codecov/Coveralls consume, so adding either service is a one-config-file follow-on.

- **Stand up GitHub Actions CI (audit TEST-02)** — `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs the full pytest suite on every push to `main`/`master` and every pull request against a Python 3.12 / 3.13 matrix (matching `requires-python = ">=3.12"`). The workflow installs the package with `pip install -e .[dev]` (zero runtime deps — install is fast and deterministic), uses `actions/setup-python@v5` with `cache: pip` keyed on `pyproject.toml`, and runs the documented dev invocation `python -m pytest tests/ -q`. `concurrency: cancel-in-progress: true` cancels superseded runs on the same ref to keep PR feedback fast and avoid burning Actions minutes. The 988-test suite that previously executed only on maintainer machines (16 PyPI releases across 0.6.x–0.7.0 with substantial per-release churn) now runs on every change — the highest-leverage single change identified by the R07.00 audit.

### Bug Fixes

- **Version git-suffix no longer misattributes foreign repositories (audit ROB-07)** — `_get_git_short_hash()` walked up to 5 parent directories looking for *any* `.git`, so a pip-installed AgentKthx that merely sat inside the *user's* project repo reported that repo's hash in its version string (observed in the wild: `0.7.00-db152d9`). Resolution order is now: (1) **live verified checkout** — a discovered `.git` is trusted only when `remote.origin.url` points at `VTSTech/AgentKthx` (https or ssh), the walk-up is capped at 2 parents, and dirty trees are explicitly marked via `git describe --always --dirty` (`0.7.00-acf1d72-dirty` means "not exactly what's on GitHub"); (2) **commit baked at build time** — new `setup.py` build hooks write `agentkthx/_git_meta.py` (`SOURCE_COMMIT = "<sha>"`) into every wheel and sdist, so PyPI installs and `pip install git+https://…` installs report the exact commit they were built from (pip's temporary clone is discarded after the wheel is built — baking is the only way the hash survives); (3) plain version. The generated file is auto-removed from the working tree after sdist builds and is .gitignored. Side benefit: typical pip installs now resolve the suffix with zero subprocess calls. 17 new tests in `tests/test_git_meta.py`; suite 963 → **980 passed / 9 skipped**.

### Maintainability

- **Strip UTF-8 BOMs from `agentkthx/skills/` + enforce no-BOM invariant (audit MAINT-06)** — 15 .py files under `agentkthx/skills/` (the package's `__init__.py` plus 14 files under `skill-creator/scripts/`) began with a UTF-8 byte-order mark (EF BB BF). Python itself executed them fine, but `ast.parse` failed on them until reads were switched to `encoding="utf-8-sig"`, and naive byte-greps silently mis-anchored matches — both failures actually bit the R07.00 dead-code analysis. Since these files ship in the wheel, any downstream contributor running linters, cross-referencers, or codemods against an installed AgentKthx hit the same friction. Fixed in three layers: (1) the 15 BOMs were stripped in place via a one-time persisted `strip_bom_in_skills.py` script (idempotent — re-running on already-clean files is a no-op, so it doubles as a verification step); (2) `EXCLUDED_DIRS` was hoisted from a local variable inside `package_skill.package_skill()` to a module-level constant so it could be shared between the new BOM scan and the existing zip write loop — they must agree on what "in the skill" means; (3) a new `_find_bom_python_files()` helper in `package_skill.py` scans every `.py` file about to be packaged, and if any begins with `bytes([0xEF, 0xBB, 0xBF])`, the packager refuses to produce a `.skill` archive and prints a clear `sed -i '1s/^\xef\xbb\xbf//' <file>` fix message — forcing the source to be fixed rather than silently stripping (silent stripping would let the issue recur on the next save). Four new regression tests in `tests/test_no_bom_in_skills.py` assert (a) no shipped `.py` under `skills/` begins with the BOM, (b) the packager rejects a BOM-bearing skill, and (c) the packager still accepts clean skills. Suite 980 → **984 passed / 9 skipped**.

## [R07.00] - 2026-09-25 12:05:00 PM

Pure-reorganization release executing `docs/R07.00-MODULARIZATION-PLAN.md`: `agent.py` (3466 lines) and `cli.py` (4270 lines) decomposed into focused modules with **zero behavioral changes** — every commit shipped with the full suite green (933 tests at baseline, 971 at completion). Closes audit findings MAINT-01 (cli.py monolith) and MAINT-04 (dual agentic loop).

Dead-code cleanup + the first R07.00-flagged bug fix. Every removal below was verified as having **zero callers repo-wide** (source, tests, docs, scripts — including string-literal references for dynamic-dispatch patterns) before deletion; the full 972-test suite ran green after every batch. Net: **−1,609 lines deleted, +163 added** (new tests + inline docs), across 34 files.

Follow-up change in the same release: the update-check cache was removed — update checks now always use the latest live PyPI/GitHub results (see Changed).

### Changed

- **Update check is now always live — the on-disk cache is gone** — `check_for_update()` previously throttled itself via `~/.agentkthx/update_check.json` (1h success / 15min negative TTLs, R06.57). The cache kept hiding freshly-cut releases from the developer — the one user who needs the live answer most — so it was removed entirely: every invocation now queries PyPI + GitHub main directly and returns whatever is live right now. Failed sources still fail independently and silently, and are simply retried live on the next invocation (no negative cache). The per-process stash in `cli/banner.py` is unchanged — still one network round per `agentkthx` process, reused for the chat-banner + post-run notices. Offline cost: at most one timeout (1s) per source, once per process; opt out with `AGENTKTHX_NO_UPDATE_CHECK=1`. A stale `update_check.json` left by an older install is ignored (safe to delete). API notes: `check_for_update()` loses its `force`/`cache_file` parameters, the result loses the now-meaningless `"source"` field (it could only ever be `"network"`), and the update check no longer writes anything under `~/.agentkthx/`.
- **`agentkthx version --refresh` retired** — the flag existed only to bypass the update-check cache (R06.57). With the cache gone there is nothing left to bypass; `agentkthx version` is always fresh now.

### Bug Fixes

- **`run_stream()` Ctrl+C-during-tool-exec NameError (PROGRESS-R07.00 flagged quirk #1)** — the KeyboardInterrupt path in `core/streaming.py` referenced the undefined `ResponseStateEvent` (survived verbatim from the original `agent.py:1499` through the R07.00 Phase 6 move). Ctrl+C while a tool executed in streaming mode raised `NameError` instead of emitting the cancellation event. Now constructs `ResponseEvent(type=EventType.RESPONSE_FAILED, ...)`, matching the fail_event pattern used by every other failure path. Regression test added (`test_streaming_subsystem.py::test_run_stream_keyboardinterrupt_during_tool_yields_response_failed` — verified to fail on the old code).

### Architecture

- **Phase 5 — unified agentic loop, `core/agentic_loop.py` (the MAINT-04 flagship)** — the near-identical `_run_core` (~571 lines) and `_run_core_streaming` (~464 lines) loop bodies are replaced by ONE loop: `_run_loop_iteration()` with `_execute_single_tool_call()` and `_process_tool_result()`. Per-path differences are now explicit and data-driven via the new `LoopCallbacks` dataclass — four streaming-only hooks (`on_step_start`: preventive compaction; `on_generated`: token snapshot + footer refresh; `on_tool_executed`: the R06.55 inline `[N] tool` print; `on_tool_result_committed`: the R06.58 between-calls compaction) plus two behavioral toggles preserved exactly as before (`include_format_hint` T/F, `mark_response_completed` T/F — streaming never marked the Response COMPLETED; preserved, documented, and test-asserted). `_run_core`/`_run_core_streaming` are now thin wrappers. Debug output is unified to the non-streaming superset, closing the 29-check debug divergence MAINT-04 had tracked since R04.
- **Phase 6 — streaming machinery, `core/streaming.py` (858 lines)** — `run_stream()` (OpenResponses SSE event generator), `_generate_stream_chunks()` and `_generate_stream()` (SSE chunk parsing + tool-call accumulation) moved verbatim into `StreamingMixin`.
- **Phase 7 — compaction subsystem, `core/compaction.py` (199 lines)** — `_check_compaction()` and `_snapshot_running_tokens()` moved verbatim; the token-tracking block became `_update_running_tokens()`.
- **Phase 9 — agent setup, `core/agent_setup.py` (520 lines)** — the ~400-line Agent constructor and `_build_default_prompt()` moved verbatim into `AgentSetupMixin`; 13 dead imports dropped from `agent.py`.
- **Phase 10 — tool execution, `core/tool_execution.py` (103 lines)** — `_execute_tool()` (registry lookup, dangerous-tool confirmation gate, argument normalization, execution, error formatting) moved verbatim into `ToolExecutionMixin`.
- **Phase 8 — `cli.py` (4270 lines) → `agentkthx/cli/` package (23 files)** — shared machinery in 8 top-level modules (`parser.py`: `create_parser`; `agent_factory.py`: `_build_agent`/`_init_acp`/skill loading; `banner.py`; `headers.py`: header/summary printers — the plan's `footer.py`, renamed since it also prints one-shot run headers; `utils.py`; `main.py`: dispatch + plugin wiring; `__main__.py`), plus one module per subcommand under `commands/` (14 modules). `cli/__init__.py` is a **compatibility facade**: it re-exports every module-level name that existed on the old module, so `from agentkthx.cli import X` and `monkeypatch.setattr(cli, 'X', ...)` keep working unchanged; the four cross-command collaborators (`_build_agent`, `_init_acp`, `_print_session_header`, `_print_update_notice`) are resolved through the facade at call time, so patching affects all consumers exactly as pre-split. 12 dead top-level imports dropped. Documented deviation: `commands/chat.py` is 1201 lines (above the plan's 800-line ceiling) — extracting the footer machinery (closures with nonlocal state) would have been a rewrite, violating the "extract, don't rewrite" principle; deferred.
- **`agent.py`: 3466 → 1096 lines (−68%)** — the Agent class is now a facade composing the five mixins (`Agent(AgentSetupMixin, CompactionMixin, ToolExecutionMixin, StreamingMixin, AgenticLoopMixin)`); public API frozen. Documented deviation from the plan's ~300-line stretch target: the MAINT-04 Phase 1-4 helpers were outside the phase spec and remain in `agent.py` (candidate for R07.01).
- **Preserved verbatim (pre-existing quirks, flagged for R07.0x)**: `run_stream()`'s KeyboardInterrupt path references an undefined `ResponseStateEvent` (would NameError); the streaming path never marks the Response COMPLETED; the streaming wrapper resets running-token counters at run start while non-streaming does not.

### Packaging

- **`requires-python`: `>=3.9` → `>=3.12`** — aligns the declared floor with reality: the CLI package (and the old `cli.py` before it) uses Python 3.12-only f-string syntax. The mismatch surfaced during the R07.00 split; the declaration is now honest.

### Documentation

- **`docs/ARCH.md` updated for R07.00** — new module map (5 core mixins, `backends/openai_compat.py` + deprecated `backends/bitnet.py` entries, full `cli/` package tree), an Agent mixin-architecture section with the `LoopCallbacks` hook/toggle table, and a CLI package layout section documenting the facade patch-compatibility contract.
- **`docs/R07.00-MODULARIZATION-PLAN.md`** — status flipped Draft → Executed.
- **`PROGRESS-R07.00.md`** — finalized to reflect all 6 phases complete (was a mid-flight snapshot that predated Phase 8).

### Removed — dead modules

- **`core/math_prompts.py` deleted (383 lines)** — the entire module had zero imports repo-wide. The live calculator tool is `tools/builtins.py`'s registered `calculator`; the GSM8K/benchmark examples each carry their own local `extract_number` copies. `docs/ARCH.md` tree updated.

### Removed — dead functions & methods (zero callers, zero tests, zero live-doc references)

- **ACP plugin: 20 methods (−455 lines)** — `_request_with_retry`, `a2a_broadcast`, `a2a_get_history`, `a2a_get_inbox`, `a2a_heartbeat`, `a2a_get_agent`, `a2a_get_agents`, `a2a_acknowledge`, `a2a_clear`, `toggle_todo`, `get_todos`, `clear_completed_todos`, `is_shutdown_nudge`, `get_duration_stats`, `add_note`, `check_budget`, `get_agent_tokens`, `log_user_message`, `get_context_id`, `get_agent_card`. Runtime only ever uses `bootstrap`, `log_chat`, `log_assistant_message`, `a2a_register`/`a2a_unregister`, `_log`, `shutdown`. The `_BatchContext` activity API (`add_read`/`add_write`/`add_edit`/`add_bash`/`add_search`/`add_api`) is **kept** — it's documented public API (ARCH Batch Context Manager).
- **`core/tool_parse.py`: 5 legacy helpers (−198 lines)** — `_fuzzy_match_tool_name` (100 lines; superseded by the live `tools/registry.py get_fuzzy()` + `core/helpers.py fuzzy_match()` pair), `_looks_like_tool_schema`, `_looks_like_tool_schema_dump`, `_extract_python_code`, `has_tool_call`.
- **`agent_mode.py`: 7 methods (−94 lines)** — `Plan.advance()` (orphaned by the R06.58 fix, which syncs `current_step_index` directly in `_execute_step`; the explanatory comment was updated), `get_rollback_point`, `queue_message`, `process_queue`, `get_progress`, `get_plan`, `get_logs`.
- **`skills/loader.py`: 7 methods (−105 lines)** — `Skill.check_compatibility`, `get_script`, `get_reference`, `get_asset`, `to_system_prompt` (the live path is `SkillRegistry.to_system_prompt_addition()`), `SkillRegistry.get_resource_path`, `get_skill_info`.
- **Backends (−70 lines)** — `BaseBackend.count_tokens`, `BackendConfig.retry_delay` (never-read field), `OllamaBackend.get_model_context_size` + `pull_model`, `OpenAICompatibleBackend.get_model_context_size`, `OllamaModel.turbo_compatible`/`turbo_note` properties.
- **`config.py` (−29 lines)** — `set_config`, `Config.ollama_host`/`ollama_port` properties, `Config.from_file`, and the three never-read mirror fields `llama_server_base_url`/`bitnet_base_url`/`zai_base_url` (plugins read the env constants directly).
- **Smaller removals** — `orchestrator.print_summary`/`list_agents` (ARCH example updated), `shared_args.SharedConfig.model_options`, `core/openresponses.Response.get_final_answer`, `core/tool_cache.clear_tool_cache`/`list_cached_models`/`get_cache_age` (ARCH Cache API section updated), `core/helpers.truncate`/`strip_code_blocks`, `core/memory.get_recent`, `core/model_config.list_supported_families`, `core/model_family_config.get_stop_sequences`, `core/error_recovery.should_suggest_alternative` + the write-only `last_success_tool` attribute (`total_failures` stays — test-asserted), `soul/loader.clear_soul_cache`/`get_allowed_tools`/`get_required_skills`/`get_optional_skills` (ARCH cache section now documents `load_soul(reload=True)`), `soul/types.is_compatible_with`, `plugins/_loader.get_all_cli_flag_choices`, `agent._log_openresponses`.
- **Kept intentionally (documented/spec API, not dead weight):** `PluginManager.unregister_hook`/`list_plugins` and the PLUGIN_SPEC-documented tool/hook API; `Agent.create_response`/`add_tool`/`get_response` (frozen public API — now test-covered, see below); the `cli/utils.py` facade-contract trio `_load_tool_cache`/`_save_tool_cache`/`_get_cloud_model_size` (existence asserted by `test_cli_package_split.py`).

### Removed — dead parameters, attributes, fields

- `_execute_tool(user_prompt=...)` — parameter was passed by both callers but never read by the body; removed from signature + both call sites (`agentic_loop.py`, `streaming.py`).
- `should_use_few_shot(model_size_hint=...)` — accepted, never used.
- `FamilyConfig`: 10 never-read dataclass fields (`tool_call_start`, `tool_call_end`, `system_prompt_style`, `reasoning_hints`, `supports_streaming`, `supports_vision`, `think_tag`, `strip_think_tags`, `needs_empty_system`, `model_size_hint`) + their construction-site kwargs in every family definition.
- `AgentSetupMixin`: write-only `self._kwargs` and `self._soul_level` initializations.

`agent.py` (5 × openresponses), `agent_mode.py` (`json`, `shutil`, `green`, `yellow`, `cyan`), `orchestrator.py` (`Any`, `Optional`), `model_discovery.py` (`DEFAULT_MODEL`), `config.py` (`Optional`), `skills/loader.py` (`os`). `pyflakes` is now clean on the package (excluding bundled skill assets).

### Tests

- 976 → **963 passed, 9 skipped, 0 failed** after the update-check change (the cleanup had brought it to 976 from 971 with +5: 1 streaming Ctrl+C regression test, 4 `Agent.create_response`/`get_response`/`add_tool` smoke tests in new `tests/test_agent_openresponses_api.py` — the frozen public OpenResponses API previously had zero coverage)
- Update-check tests: `tests/test_update_check.py` 74 → 61 items — 17 cache-behavior tests removed (TTL boundaries, fresh/stale/negative-cache hits, `force` bypass, legacy-cache migration, cache-dir creation), replaced by 4 always-live tests (every invocation hits the network; a release cut between two calls is visible immediately; failed sources are retried live, not negatively cached) + `TestCacheRemoved` removal guards (cache plumbing absent; signature has no `force`/`cache_file` params)
- The cleanup was executed in 6 batches, full suite green after each: (1) core function deletions, (2) backends/config/orchestrator, (3) ACP, (4) stragglers found by re-running the dead-code cross-referencer post-deletion (`pull_model`, `a2a_get_agents`, skills/loader getters), (5) imports/fields/params, (6) docs + version bump.
